Early winter chill: a bitter wind, low grey clouds, cold drizzle and drifting sea mist, wet stone and mud, dry brown grass and bare thorn bushes; no snow and no ice anywhere. Lighting: orange torchlight flickering against cold blue darkness, embers in the air. TRUE NIGHT: deep darkness and a black sky; faces are lit only by the nearby flame or glow, and everything outside its reach falls into black; no daylight anywhere. Mood: threatening. Faint breath vapor in the cold air. The villagers are people of different heights and builds, each in a different natural pose, their lights held at uneven, random heights. All mouths closed.
